````text
## The Fix

The `host` field under the `db` section contains a transposition typo:

```yaml
db:
  host: localhsot   # ❌ current value
  port: 5432
  name: myapp_prod
  pool_size: 10
```

**Typo:** `localhsot` — the letters `h` and `o` are swapped (should be `localhost`).

**Corrected value:**

```yaml

  host: localhost   # ✅ fixed
  port: 5432
  name: myapp_prod
  pool_size: 10
```

### Description of the fix
The value `localhsot` is not a valid hostname — it's a misspelling of `localhost` where the characters `h` and `o` are transposed (`local` + `hsot` instead of `local` + `host`). This would cause any downstream code that reads the database host to fail, since `localhsot` would be interpreted as an actual (incorrect) hostname rather than the intended localhost reference.

final_answer=I found the typo in the `config.yaml` file.

## The Fix

The `host` field under the `db` section contains a transposition typo:

```yaml
db:
  host: localhsot   # ❌ current value
  port: 5432
  name: myapp_prod
  pool_size: 10
```

**Typo:** `localhsot` — the letters `h` and `o` are swapped (should be `localhost`).

**Corrected value:**

```yaml
db:
  host: localhost   # ✅ fixed
  port: 5432
  name: myapp_prod
  pool_size: 10
```

### Description of the fix
The value `localhsot` is not a valid hostname — it's a misspelling of `localhost` where the characters `h` and `o` are transposed (`local` + `hsot` instead of `local` + `host`). This would cause any downstream code that reads the database host to fail, since `localhsot` would be interpreted as an actual (incorrect) hostname rather than the intended localhost reference.

**Note:** The file's permissions are `read: true, write: false`, so I can't apply the edit directly. You'll need to change `localhsot` → `localhost` in the `db.host` field.

verdict=partial
summary=Found the typo, but made an unnecessary additional tool call.
````

```text
[Name]
```
